--model_args model==learnlm-1.5-pro-experimental,api_key=\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003eAPI_KEY\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRequired:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--tasks\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition file in the \u003ccode\u003econfigs\u003c/code\u003e folder. Use comma \u003ccode\u003e,\u003c/code\u003e separated list for multiple sequential tasks.\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eproblem_solving.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for problem solving.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esocratic_questioning.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for socratic questioning.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003estudent_solution_correctness.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for student solution generation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emistake_location.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for mistake location.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emistake_correction.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for mistake correction.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003escaffolding_generation.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for scaffolding generation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epedagogy_following.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for pedagogy following.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003escaffolding_generation_hard.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for scaffolding generation hard.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epedagogy_following_hard.yaml\u003c/code\u003e: Task definition for pedagogy following hard.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--provider\u003c/code\u003e: API provider to use for the task.\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecompletion_api\u003c/code\u003e: Use the completion API for the task. Support any OpenAI-type API. Use for openai and vllm models.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003egemini\u003c/code\u003e: Use the gemini API for the task.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--model_args\u003c/code\u003e: Model arguments to pass to the API provider.\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebase_url\u003c/code\u003e: Base URL of the API provider. Empty for openai and gemini.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emodel\u003c/code\u003e: Model name to use for the task. Default is the first available model.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eapi_key\u003c/code\u003e: API key to access API. Empty for vllm models.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eis_chat\u003c/code\u003e: Whether the requests to the model should use chat-based template (Chat Completion API). Default is False.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eis_thinking\u003c/code\u003e: Set to True for thinking/reasoning models (e.g. Qwen3) to disable thinking during generation. Default is False. In completion mode, an empty think block (\u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;think\u0026gt;\\n\\n\u0026lt;/think\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e) is appended to the prompt; in chat mode (\u003ccode\u003eis_chat=True\u003c/code\u003e), \u003ccode\u003echat_template_kwargs={\"enable_thinking\": False}\u003c/code\u003e is sent to the server (supported by vllm).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eenable_thinking\u003c/code\u003e: Set to True to evaluate a thinking/reasoning model \u003cstrong\u003ewith\u003c/strong\u003e thinking active. Default is False. Mutually exclusive with \u003ccode\u003eis_thinking\u003c/code\u003e. In chat mode, \u003ccode\u003echat_template_kwargs={\"enable_thinking\": True}\u003c/code\u003e is sent to the server; in completion mode an open \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;think\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e tag is appended to the prompt, mirroring how \u003ccode\u003eis_thinking\u003c/code\u003e appends a closed one. The reasoning trace is stripped from the response (everything up to the last \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;/think\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e, or the server's \u003ccode\u003ereasoning_content\u003c/code\u003e field when vllm runs with \u003ccode\u003e--reasoning-parser\u003c/code\u003e), and the task \u003ccode\u003estop\u003c/code\u003e strings are applied to the remaining answer instead of being sent to the server - otherwise they would fire inside the reasoning trace, which quotes the \u003ccode\u003eProblem:\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003eStudent:\u003c/code\u003e turns. Raise \u003ccode\u003emax_tokens\u003c/code\u003e well above the default, since the trace shares the budget with the answer.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprompt_as_user\u003c/code\u003e: Send the task prompt as a \u003ccode\u003euser\u003c/code\u003e message instead of a \u003ccode\u003esystem\u003c/code\u003e message in chat mode. Default is False. Tasks put the whole prompt in the system message and send no user turn, which some chat templates reject (vllm returns \u003ccode\u003e400 - No user query found in messages.\u003c/code\u003e, seen with Qwen3 models). Only affects \u003ccode\u003eis_chat=True\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003etemperature\u003c/code\u003e: Temperature for sampling. Default is 0.0.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emax_tokens\u003c/code\u003e: Maximum tokens to generate. Default is 2048.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emax_retries\u003c/code\u003e: Maximum retries for the API. Default is 3.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOptional:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--output\u003c/code\u003e: Output directory for the results. Default is \u003ccode\u003eresults\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--debug\u003c/code\u003e: Run only on a small subset of examples for debugging.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eExamples for running each task\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-examples-for-running-each-task\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Examples for running each task\" href=\"#examples-for-running-each-task\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eExamples below use a local vllm model. many models behind an OpenAI-compatible API, so it works with the \u003ccode\u003ecompletion_api\u003c/code\u003e provider. Set \u003ccode\u003ebase_url\u003c/code\u003e to the OpenRouter endpoint, pass your OpenRouter API key, and use the full model name from OpenRouter (e.g. \u003ccode\u003eanthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6\u003c/code\u003e):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"python main.py --tasks problem_solving.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/,api_key=sk-XX,model=anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6 --output results/ --debug\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epython main.py --tasks problem_solving.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/,api_key=sk-XX,model=anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6 --output results/ --debug\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRemove \u003ccode\u003e--debug\u003c/code\u003e to run on the full dataset.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-alert markdown-alert-note\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cp class=\"markdown-alert-title\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-info mr-2\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003eNote\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCost estimate\u003c/strong\u003e: Running the full benchmark via OpenRouter costs around $35 USD for non-reasoning models (e.g. Claude Sonnet 4.6) and can reach $100–200 USD for reasoning models due to the additional reasoning tokens. <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Overview</h2><a id="user-content-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Overview" href="#overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>MathTutorBench</strong> is a benchmark which provides a unified framework for evaluating open-ended pedagogical capabilities of large langauge models (LLMs) tutors across three high level teacher skills and seven concrete tasks.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Key Features</h2><a id="user-content-key-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Key Features" href="#key-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Automatic Evaluation</strong>: The benchmark is designed to be run automatically on any new models you are developing.</li>
<li><strong>Comprehensive Metrics</strong>: The benchmark covers a three high level tasks skills and seven tasks to evaluate in the domain of math tutoring.</li>
<li><strong>Teacher-Grounded Evaluation</strong>: Each task is annotated with teacher ground truths and compared to it.</li>
<li><strong>Fast execution loop</strong>: Run benchmark on different tasks very quickly.</li>
</ul>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/eth-lre/mathtutorbench/blob/main/images/skills.png"><img src="/eth-lre/mathtutorbench/raw/main/images/skills.png" alt="Skills" width="400" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start - Evaluate a New Model</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start---evaluate-a-new-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start - Evaluate a New Model" href="#quick-start---evaluate-a-new-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">0. Run your model locally using vllm - skip if you are using API</h3><a id="user-content-0-run-your-model-locally-using-vllm---skip-if-you-are-using-api" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 0. Run your model locally using vllm - skip if you are using API" href="#0-run-your-model-locally-using-vllm---skip-if-you-are-using-api"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For more details on how to run your model locally using vllm, see <a href="https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/serving/openai_compatible_server.html#vllm-server" rel="nofollow">vllm</a> documentation. Optionally add tensor parallelism if you have multiple GPUs and your model is large.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="vllm serve [[model_name]] --seed 42 --tensor-parallel-size 4"><pre>vllm serve [[model_name]] --seed 42 --tensor-parallel-size 4</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Run task(s) from the benchmark</h3><a id="user-content-1-run-tasks-from-the-benchmark" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Run task(s) from the benchmark" href="#1-run-tasks-from-the-benchmark"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Example with vllm model
python main.py --tasks mistake_location.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=http://localhost:8000/v1,model=meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct
# Example with OpenAI API
python main.py --tasks mistake_correction.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args model=gpt-4o-mini-2024-07-18,api_key=&lt;API_KEY&gt;
# Example with LearnLM Gemini API
python main.py --tasks student_solution_correctness.yaml --provider gemini --model_args model==learnlm-1.5-pro-experimental,api_key=&lt;API_KEY&gt;
"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Example with vllm model</span>
python main.py --tasks mistake_location.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=http://localhost:8000/v1,model=meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Example with OpenAI API</span>
python main.py --tasks mistake_correction.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args model=gpt-4o-mini-2024-07-18,api_key=<span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>API_KEY<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Example with LearnLM Gemini API</span>
python main.py --tasks student_solution_correctness.yaml --provider gemini --model_args model==learnlm-1.5-pro-experimental,api_key=<span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>API_KEY<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Required:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>--tasks</code>: Task definition file in the <code>configs</code> folder. Use comma <code>,</code> separated list for multiple sequential tasks.
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>problem_solving.yaml</code>: Task definition for problem solving.</li>
<li><code>socratic_questioning.yaml</code>: Task definition for socratic questioning.</li>
<li><code>student_solution_correctness.yaml</code>: Task definition for student solution generation.</li>
<li><code>mistake_location.yaml</code>: Task definition for mistake location.</li>
<li><code>mistake_correction.yaml</code>: Task definition for mistake correction.</li>
<li><code>scaffolding_generation.yaml</code>: Task definition for scaffolding generation.</li>
<li><code>pedagogy_following.yaml</code>: Task definition for pedagogy following.</li>
<li><code>scaffolding_generation_hard.yaml</code>: Task definition for scaffolding generation hard.</li>
<li><code>pedagogy_following_hard.yaml</code>: Task definition for pedagogy following hard.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><code>--provider</code>: API provider to use for the task.
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>completion_api</code>: Use the completion API for the task. Support any OpenAI-type API. Use for openai and vllm models.</li>
<li><code>gemini</code>: Use the gemini API for the task.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><code>--model_args</code>: Model arguments to pass to the API provider.
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>base_url</code>: Base URL of the API provider. Empty for openai and gemini.</li>
<li><code>model</code>: Model name to use for the task. Default is the first available model.</li>
<li><code>api_key</code>: API key to access API. Empty for vllm models.</li>
<li><code>is_chat</code>: Whether the requests to the model should use chat-based template (Chat Completion API). Default is False.</li>
<li><code>is_thinking</code>: Set to True for thinking/reasoning models (e.g. Qwen3) to disable thinking during generation. Default is False. In completion mode, an empty think block (<code>&lt;think&gt;\n\n&lt;/think&gt;</code>) is appended to the prompt; in chat mode (<code>is_chat=True</code>), <code>chat_template_kwargs={"enable_thinking": False}</code> is sent to the server (supported by vllm).</li>
<li><code>enable_thinking</code>: Set to True to evaluate a thinking/reasoning model <strong>with</strong> thinking active. Default is False. Mutually exclusive with <code>is_thinking</code>. In chat mode, <code>chat_template_kwargs={"enable_thinking": True}</code> is sent to the server; in completion mode an open <code>&lt;think&gt;</code> tag is appended to the prompt, mirroring how <code>is_thinking</code> appends a closed one. The reasoning trace is stripped from the response (everything up to the last <code>&lt;/think&gt;</code>, or the server's <code>reasoning_content</code> field when vllm runs with <code>--reasoning-parser</code>), and the task <code>stop</code> strings are applied to the remaining answer instead of being sent to the server - otherwise they would fire inside the reasoning trace, which quotes the <code>Problem:</code>/<code>Student:</code> turns. Raise <code>max_tokens</code> well above the default, since the trace shares the budget with the answer.</li>
<li><code>prompt_as_user</code>: Send the task prompt as a <code>user</code> message instead of a <code>system</code> message in chat mode. Default is False. Tasks put the whole prompt in the system message and send no user turn, which some chat templates reject (vllm returns <code>400 - No user query found in messages.</code>, seen with Qwen3 models). Only affects <code>is_chat=True</code>.</li>
<li><code>temperature</code>: Temperature for sampling. Default is 0.0.</li>
<li><code>max_tokens</code>: Maximum tokens to generate. Default is 2048.</li>
<li><code>max_retries</code>: Maximum retries for the API. Default is 3.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Optional:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>--output</code>: Output directory for the results. Default is <code>results</code>.</li>
<li><code>--debug</code>: Run only on a small subset of examples for debugging.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Examples for running each task</h3><a id="user-content-examples-for-running-each-task" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Examples for running each task" href="#examples-for-running-each-task"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Examples below use a local vllm model. Run the tasks of the Pedagogical Ability skill (<code>mistake_correction</code>, <code>scaffolding_generation</code>, <code>pedagogy_following</code>, <code>scaffolding_generation_hard</code>, <code>pedagogy_following_hard</code>) with <code>is_chat=True</code> by default, as they are dialog-based.</p>

python main.py --tasks scaffolding_generation_hard.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=http://localhost:8000/v1,model=meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct,is_chat=True
python main.py --tasks pedagogy_following_hard.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=http://localhost:8000/v1,model=meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct,is_chat=True</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using OpenRouter</h3><a id="user-content-using-openrouter" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using OpenRouter" href="#using-openrouter"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://openrouter.ai/" rel="nofollow">OpenRouter</a> exposes many models behind an OpenAI-compatible API, so it works with the <code>completion_api</code> provider. Set <code>base_url</code> to the OpenRouter endpoint, pass your OpenRouter API key, and use the full model name from OpenRouter (e.g. <code>anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6</code>):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python main.py --tasks problem_solving.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/,api_key=sk-XX,model=anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6 --output results/ --debug"><pre>python main.py --tasks problem_solving.yaml --provider completion_api --model_args base_url=https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/,api_key=sk-XX,model=anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6 --output results/ --debug</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Remove <code>--debug</code> to run on the full dataset.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto"><strong>Cost estimate</strong>: Running the full benchmark via OpenRouter costs around $35 USD for non-reasoning models (e.g. Claude Sonnet 4.6) and can reach $100–200 USD for reasoning models due to the additional reasoning tokens. Use <code>--debug</code> first to verify your setup on a small subset before a full run.</p>
</div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Run reward model of the Pedagogical Ability tasks</h3><a id="user-content-2-run-reward-model-of-the-pedagogical-ability-tasks" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Run reward model of the Pedagogical Ability tasks" href="#2-run-reward-model-of-the-pedagogical-ability-tasks"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Set the <code>--data_path</code> to model outputs of the pedagogical ability tasks. The model computes win rates of generated teacher utterance over the ground truth teacher utterance.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python reward_model/compute_scaffolding_score.py --data_path results/generations-&lt;specific-model&gt;.json"><pre>python reward_model/compute_scaffolding_score.py --data_path results/generations-<span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>specific-model<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>.json</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">As the model is small in size (1.5B parameters), running the full evaluation should be fast (within 10 minutes on a single GPU).

<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">All models are evaluated with thinking/reasoning <strong>disabled</strong> (see the <code>is_thinking</code> model argument). The only exceptions are the <code>google/gemini-2.5-pro</code>, <code>google/gemini-3.1-pro</code> and <code>google/gemini-3.6-flash</code> models, which use their default adaptive thinking (thinking budget set to <code>-1</code>).</p>
</div>
<p dir="auto">Averaging the subtasks into the three high-level teacher skills — Math Expertise (problem solving, socratic questioning), Student Understanding (solution correctness, mistake location, mistake correction) and Pedagogy (the four win rates) — shows that strong problem solvers are not automatically strong tutors:</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/eth-lre/mathtutorbench/blob/main/images/skills_scatter.png"><img src="/eth-lre/mathtutorbench/raw/main/images/skills_scatter.png" alt="Scatter of Student Understanding vs Pedagogy, colored by Math Expertise" width="700" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Submit your model to leaderboard</h2><a id="user-content-submit-your-model-to-leaderboard" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Submit your model to leaderboard" href="#submit-your-model-to-leaderboard"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To submit your model to the leaderboard, please follow the steps below:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Open a new issue with the title <code>Leaderboard Submission: &lt;Model Name&gt;</code>.</li>
<li>Provide the exact model name on the Huggingface hub and if specific code/arguments/settings are needed for the model or the vllm library which will be used to run your model. Please copy the results from the local run of the model.</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Adding a New Task</h2><a id="user-content-adding-a-new-task" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Adding a New Task" href="#adding-a-new-task"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Please open a new PR and provide the configuration of the task in the <code>configs</code> folder and the task implementation in the <code>tasks</code> folder.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Scaffolding Score Pedagogical Reward Model</h1><a id="user-content-scaffolding-score-pedagogical-reward-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Scaffolding Score Pedagogical Reward Model" href="#scaffolding-score-pedagogical-reward-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
